# AndroCAD Engineering Roadmap & Milestone Ledger

This document tracks development milestones, architectural decisions, and open risks for **AndroCAD**. It is updated and committed at every milestone transition.

---

## Architectural Decisions

1. **OCCT Version Selection: Upgraded to OCCT 8.0.1 (`V8_0_1`)**
   * *Comparison (7.8.0 vs 8.0.1):* Open CASCADE Technology was originally pinned to `V7_8_0` and is now upgraded to **`V8_0_1` (OCCT 8.0.1)** (`LGPL-2.1-only WITH OCCT-exception-1.0`), incorporating C++17 `occ::handle` / `NCollection` modernizations and modular Data Exchange (`TKDE`, `TKDESTEP`, `TKDESTL`, `TKXSBase`) on Android NDK r26d (`arm64-v8a`, 16 KB page alignment).
2. **Sketch Constraint Solver: PlaneGCS**
   * *Selection:* FreeCAD's PlaneGCS solver (`LGPL-2.1-or-later`) written in C++. It uses Levenberg-Marquardt and Dogleg nonlinear solvers, compiles with the same Android NDK toolchain, and covers all required 2D sketch constraints. SolveSpace (`libslvs`) was strictly rejected due to its copyleft `GPL-3.0-or-later` license.
3. **Rendering Architecture: Native C++ OpenGL ES 3.x First (Vulkan Backend Follow-up)**
   * *Selection:* Native C++ rendering loop interfacing with Android `SurfaceView` / `ANativeWindow`. Mesh buffers (`Poly_Triangulation`) generated by OCCT `BRepMesh` are written directly into native GPU Vertex Buffer Objects (VBOs), completely bypassing the Java/Kotlin Virtual Machine to eliminate garbage collection pauses.
4. **Proprietary Application with Dynamic LGPL Linkage**
   * *Selection:* Application Kotlin/Compose code is proprietary (All Rights Reserved). OCCT and PlaneGCS are built strictly as dynamically linked `.so` libraries. Relinking instructions and build scripts are published. *Flagged: Needs legal review before commercial release.*
5. **Compiler Flags Strategy**
   * *Selection:* Rather than blanket `-Oz` (which degrades numerical performance in heavy math loops), compute-heavy toolkits (`TKBO`, `TKBool`, `TKFillet`, `TKMesh`, `TKGeomAlgo`) are compiled with `-O2 -flto`, while foundational and data exchange toolkits use `-Oz` for size reduction.
6. **Storage Access Framework (SAF) vs. Broad Storage Permissions**
   * *Selection:* AndroCAD strictly adheres to modern Android privacy best practices by rejecting broad file permissions (`READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`, `WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`, and `MANAGE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE`). No storage permissions are requested at application startup. Instead, the application exclusively employs Android's Storage Access Framework (`ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument` and `ActivityResultContracts.CreateDocument`) on-demand when the user initiates an Import or Export action.
   * *Content URI Bridge:* Native C++ Open CASCADE (OCCT) file I/O routines cannot directly access Android `content://` URIs. On import, the content stream is copied safely into application cache (`context.cacheDir`), passed to the native kernel as a valid POSIX path, and cleaned up after mesh ingestion. On export, the native kernel exports to a temporary cache file, which is streamed directly into the SAF document output stream.
   * *Empty State:* First-time users are greeted with a clear, welcoming empty-state screen offering direct access to the Onboarding Tutorial, Sample CAD Designs gallery, and file import, ensuring immediate engagement without scary permission dialogs.
7. **Interactive Touch Sketching & Fusion 360-Style Parametric Extrude Workflow**
   * *Selection:* To provide an intuitive direct manipulation CAD experience akin to Fusion 360, 2D sketch input is decoupled from raw camera touch manipulation via a specialized Compose overlay (`SketchCanvasOverlay`) positioned above the GLSurfaceView viewport when the Sketch workspace is active.
   * *Direct Manipulation:* 1-finger drag operations draw parametric primitives (Rectangles, Circles, Polylines) with live rubber-band stroke rendering, dynamic dimension tags, and automatic closed-profile detection with translucent cyan shading (`#3300A4E4`).
   * *Parametric Extrude Inspector:* Finishing a sketch automatically transitions the camera smoothly to 3D isometric (`NamedView.HOME`) and displays a floating parameter inspector (`ExtrudeInspectorDialog`). Dragging the depth slider immediately recalculates the B-Rep extrusion in Open CASCADE and pushes the updated mesh to the GLES viewport in real time. Committing creates an associative solid body in the Browser tree and appends an entry to the Parametric Timeline.
8. **Magnetic Vertex Snapping, Topological Point Welding & True Solid Primitives**
   * *Magnetic Snapping:* Touch drawing on mobile screens inherently lacks sub-millimeter precision. A 7.0 mm magnetic vertex snap zone (`findNearbyVertex`) locks drag start and end points to existing line endpoints, rectangle corners, and circle centers, with visual yellow indicator rings.
   * *Topological Point Welding:* Unwelded coincident endpoints in OpenCASCADE cause `BRepBuilderAPI_MakeWire` to fail loop closure. The bridge applies 0.2 mm quantized point deduplication (`pointMap`), ensuring shared vertices reference identical OpenCASCADE point indices.
   * *True Sketch-Driven Extrude & Revolve:* Both Extrude and Revolve now assemble and extrude/revolve the actual user-drawn sketch entities rather than static milestone demo shapes. Fallback sizing dynamically measures the bounding box of the active sketch.
   * *Direct Modeling Workflow:* Added 1-tap `+ Sketch` (`btn_solid_create_sketch`) button in the Solid ribbon, a floating direct Extrude action button upon closed-profile detection, a true parametric Cylinder primitive (`tool_Cylinder`), and a clean-slate "✨ NEW DESIGN" button (`btn_new_design`).

---

## Open Technical & Architectural Risks

| Risk Area | Description | Mitigation Strategy |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Topological Naming Problem (TNP)** | Parametric recomputation modifies topological entity indices, breaking downstream sketches/fillets. | 3-tier mitigation: Semantic origin tagging -> `BRepTools_History` tracking -> Invariant geometric fingerprint fallback. Dedicated regression suite written in Milestone 5. |
| **LGPL Relinking on Android** | LGPL 2.1 Section 6 requires allowing users to relink or replace the LGPL library. | Deliver OCCT and PlaneGCS as external `.so` libraries in `lib/<abi>/`. Provide automated Docker / GitHub Actions script allowing users to compile replacement `.so` files and resign the APK. |
| **OCCT Android Build Size** | Full OCCT is >50 MB per ABI, exceeding typical mobile app budgets. | Prune visualization, Draw, and OCAF toolkits (`BUILD_MODULE_Visualization=OFF`). Strip debug symbols, apply LTO and dead-code elimination. |
| **Boolean & Fillet Robustness** | OCCT BOPAlgo and ChFi3d rolling ball algorithms can fail on near-coincident faces or complex corners. | Use `BRepAlgoAPI::SetFuzzyValue(1e-5)` for booleans. Wrap all operations with `IsDone()` validation and graceful C++ `Standard_Failure` exception boundaries. |

## Milestones Checklist

### Milestone 1: OCCT Android Build & JNI Smoke Test
* **Goal:** Cross-compile pruned OCCT 7.8.0 for Android (`arm64-v8a`), create a JNI smoke test that instantiates a solid box, tessellates it via `BRepMesh`, and renders it via native GLES. Measure real `.so` and APK sizes.
* **Deliverables:**
  - GitHub Actions workflow `occt-build.yml` caching pruned OCCT binaries.
  - Android Gradle + CMake project with JNI bridge (`libandrocad_kernel.so`).
  - Host-side CTest/GoogleTest smoke test generating and validating a solid box.
  - Fix feature-mapping table and document TNP regression suite plan.
  - Milestone report with measured sizes.
* **Acceptance Tests:** Host tests pass in CI; Android debug APK builds and runs JNI smoke test without crashes.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-19

---

### Milestone 2: Native Viewport & Picking Engine
* **Goal:** Interactive 3D viewport on Android: orbit, pan, zoom, ViewCube, display modes (shaded, shaded+edges, wireframe), and $O(1)$ GPU color-coded face and edge picking.
* **Deliverables:** Native GLES 3.x render loop, ViewCube widget, touch gesture recognizer (single/two finger), color-buffer picking pass.
* **Acceptance Tests:** Smooth 60+ FPS touch manipulation; accurate face and edge selection.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-20

---

### Milestone 3: Sketcher with PlaneGCS
* **Goal:** 2D parametric sketcher on datum planes and planar faces with pencil/touch snapping and constraint solving.
* **Deliverables:** Primitives (line, arc, circle, rectangle, spline), constraints (coincident, horizontal, vertical, parallel, perpendicular, tangent, equal, dimension), profile loop detection.
* **Acceptance Tests:** Over/under-constrained status indicators; profile closes into valid `TopoDS_Face`.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-20

## Post-M7 Fusion 360 Parity & Autonomous Expansion (Milestones 8 - 15)

With the foundation established (Milestones 1–7), the roadmap expands toward professional engineering parity with Fusion 360 workflows. Each milestone produces a public/private GitHub Release with an attached **Beta APK** (`app-debug.apk`), continuing autonomously without blocking on user confirmations.

---

### Milestone 8: Advanced Surface Modeling & Direct Editing
* **Goal:** Expand beyond basic prismatic solids into complex freeform surface modeling and direct topology modification (Fusion 360 Surface workspace parity).
* **Deliverables:**
  - **Loft:** Cross-sectional profile lofting (`BRepOffsetAPI_MakeLoft`) with periodic and ruled surface flags.
  - **Sweep:** Trajectory pipe and spine guide sweep (`BRepOffsetAPI_MakePipe` / `BRepOffsetAPI_MakePipeShell`).
  - **Surface Patch & Sewing:** Boundary curve patching and watertight sewing (`BRepBuilderAPI_Sewing`) transforming composite shells into solid manifolds.
  - **Thicken:** Uniform shell thickening (`BRepOffsetAPI_MakeThicken`) converting open surfaces to solid geometry.
  - **Direct Modeling:** Face offset / Press-Pull and face defeaturing/removal.
  - **Host CTest Suite:** `test_surface_ops.cpp` verifying surface continuity, volume, and sewing tolerances.
  - **UI Integration:** Surface workspace ribbon tools and property parameters.
  - **Release:** Beta APK `v0.8.0-beta`.
* **Acceptance Tests:** Complex multi-section loft and pipe sweep pass regression; stitched surface produces valid solid verified via `BRepCheck_Analyzer`.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-20

---

### Milestone 9: Multi-Component Assemblies & Kinematic Joints
* **Goal:** Multi-body assembly architecture with real-time kinematic degree-of-freedom (DoF) constraint solving (Fusion 360 Assemble workspace parity).
* **Deliverables:**
  - Component tree architecture with independent coordinate transformations (`TopLoc_Location` / `gp_Trsf`).
  - Kinematic Joint primitives: Rigid, Revolute (rotational limits $[-\theta, +\theta]$), Slider (prismatic displacement bounds), Cylindrical, Ball-and-Socket.
  - Interactive joint motion solver and animated simulation player.
  - Collision interference detection (`BRepAlgoAPI_Section`).
  - **Release:** Beta APK `v0.9.0-beta`.
* **Acceptance Tests:** Two-link kinematic arm with revolute and slider joints solves and animates within angular bounds without joint divergence.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-21

---

### Milestone 10: Parametric Sheet Metal & Flat Pattern Generation
* **Goal:** Specialized sheet metal manufacturing workflow with automatic flat pattern unwrapping (Fusion 360 Sheet Metal workspace parity).
* **Deliverables:**
  - Sheet metal rules (material thickness $t$, bend radius $R$, neutral axis K-factor $K \approx 0.44$).
  - Flange tool (edge bend extrusion), Bend Relief cuts.
  - Unfold engine computing 2D flat patterns with bend centerlines (Up/Down direction and bend angles).
  - 2D DXF manufacturing export for laser/waterjet cutting.
  - **Release:** Beta APK `v0.10.0-beta`.
* **Acceptance Tests:** U-bracket with two $90^\circ$ flanges unfolds to accurate flat sheet area matching analytical K-factor bend deduction.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-21

---

### Milestone 11: 2D Engineering Drawings & Drafting Sheet
* **Goal:** Automated 2D drafting with Hidden Line Removal (HLR) projection onto engineering drawing sheets (Fusion 360 Drawing workspace parity).
* **Deliverables:**
  - OCCT projection engine producing visible and hidden line vectors.
  - Standard drawing views: Front, Top, Right, Isometric (ANSI / ISO 3rd Angle).
  - Section Cut view generation along custom cutting planes with 45° cross-hatching.
  - Dimension annotations, centerlines, and standard title block layout.
  - Vector SVG and AutoCAD DXF export of drawing sheets.
  - **Release:** Beta APK `v0.11.0-beta`.
* **Acceptance Tests:** Stepped shaft and bracket models produce complete 4-view drawing sheet with accurate HLR hidden dashed lines and vector SVG/DXF export.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-21

---

### Milestone 12: Materials, Mass Physical Properties & PBR Visualization
* **Goal:** Engineering materials physics, mass moment calculations, and photorealistic PBR rendering (Fusion 360 Render & Inspect parity).
* **Deliverables:**
  - Material database (Steel, 6061-T6 Aluminum, Brass, Titanium, ABS, Polycarbonate, Oak) with density, Young's modulus, and Poisson's ratio.
  - Mass properties engine (`BRepGProp`): precise volume, mass, Center of Gravity (CoG), and principal moments of inertia ($I_{xx}, I_{yy}, I_{zz}$).
  - Physically Based Rendering (PBR) shader pipeline: Metallic-Roughness, normal mapping, and Image-Based Lighting (IBL) environment reflection.
  - High-res raytraced / rasterized snapshot export.
  - **Release:** Beta APK `v0.12.0-beta`.
* **Acceptance Tests:** Calculated mass and center of gravity match analytical physics values within $0.05\%$; PBR metallic preview renders with environment reflection.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-21

  - Mesh-to-solid watertight conversion via OCCT `BRepBuilderAPI_Sewing` and `BRepBuilderAPI_MakeSolid`.
  - **Release:** Beta APK `v0.13.0-beta`.
* **Acceptance Tests:** High-density scanned mesh successfully analyzed, repaired, smoothed, and converted into solid manifold B-Rep.

* **Acceptance Tests:** Cantilever beam under point load matches theoretical Euler-Bernoulli deflection, stress distribution, and factor of safety.

## Faz 3: Post-1.0 Hardening & Expansion

### Milestone 21: JNI Export & Signature Parity Audit (Name-Mangling Regression Guard)
* **Goal:** Prevent Kotlin `internal external fun` bytecode name-mangling and missing C++ `JNIEXPORT` symbols (v1.0.2 device crash class) via static and runtime JNI symbol table verification.
* **Deliverables:**
  - Automated C++ and Kotlin JNI signature parser (`JniSymbolAuditor`) verifying 1:1 correspondence between `NativeKernel.kt` `external fun` declarations and `native_kernel_jni.cpp` `Java_com_androcad_kernel_NativeKernel_*` exports.
  - Zero `internal external fun` enforcement check preventing `$core_kernel_debug` symbol mangling.
  - Host CTest executable `androcad_faz3_m21_test` and JUnit test `JniExportAuditTest`.
  - Tablet landscape (`docs/screenshots/m21_tablet.png`) and phone portrait (`docs/screenshots/m21_phone.png`) verified diagnostic screenshots.
  - Release: `v1.0.12`.
* **Acceptance Tests:** 100% of declared Kotlin `external fun` methods resolve to exact C++ `JNIEXPORT` symbols with matching parameter signatures; CTest passes.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-24

---

### Milestone 22: EGL/GL Thread Safety & Render Context Guard
* **Goal:** Prevent UI-thread OpenGL ES calls (`GL_INVALID_OPERATION` black viewport regression from v1.0.1 on Xiaomi Pad 6) through compile-time and runtime thread-affinity guards.
* **Deliverables:**
  - `GlThreadGuard` engine tracking thread IDs of EGLContext creation vs. VBO/shader upload calls and enforcing deferred command queue execution on the GL thread.
  - Thread-safe ring-buffer command queue for mesh upload, secondary body buffers, and camera state mutations in `CadRenderer.kt`.
  - Host CTest executable `androcad_faz3_m22_test` simulating concurrent UI-thread mesh updates and GL-thread frame draws.
  - Tablet (`docs/screenshots/m22_tablet.png`) and phone (`docs/screenshots/m22_phone.png`) screenshots.
  - Release: `v1.0.13`.
* **Acceptance Tests:** Zero GL buffer or shader operations execute outside the bound render thread under 1,000 concurrent UI mutation events.
* **Status:** Done
* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-24

* **Deliverables:**
  - `SoDependencyGraph` C++ analyzer validating topological load order of `libc++_shared.so`, 48 OCCT toolkits, and `libandrocad_kernel.so` across all 3 Android ABIs.
  - Cycle detector and missing transitive symbol verifier prior to `System.loadLibrary` invocation.
  - Host CTest `androcad_faz3_m23_test` verifying topological sort invariants for all 49 shared libraries.
  - Tablet (`docs/screenshots/m23_tablet.png`) and phone (`docs/screenshots/m23_phone.png`) screenshots.
  - Release: `v1.0.14`.
* **Acceptance Tests:** Every `.so` library in the 49-library graph loads only after 100% of its `DT_NEEDED` prerequisites are satisfied across `arm64-v8a`, `armeabi-v7a`, and `x86_64`.

* **Finish Date:** 2026-10-24

## Faz 4: Industrial CAD/CAM/CAE Mastery & 3D Direct Manipulation Verification (Milestones 71–270)

> **Faz 4 Scope:** 200 new milestones (`Milestone 71` through `Milestone 270`) delivering:
> 1. **3D Direct Manipulation & Ray-Edge-Face Target Resolution (`M71`–`M90`):** Unifies OpenGL ES 3.0 viewport and OpenCascade 7.8.0 into true CAD **Z-Up** (`XY` ground plane, `+Z` vertical axis), prioritizes distance-scaled 3D ray-edge picking over clamped 1px mobile FBO lines, and resolves exact 3D `selectedHitPoint` & `selectedFaceNormal` targets so tapping the top of a 3D model always operates on the top edge/face and never falls back to bottom `Edge #1`.
> 2. **180 Industrial CAD/CAM/CAE Expansion Milestones (`M91`–`M270`):** Advanced Parametric Sketching & Conics (`M91`–`M110`), Synchronous Direct Editing & Enclosures (`M111`–`M130`), Class-A Surfacing, Subdivision & Point-Cloud Reverse Engineering (`M131`–`M150`), Sheet Metal Nesting, Weldments & ASME Piping (`M151`–`M170`), Multibody Dynamics, Inverse Kinematics & Six-Sigma Tolerance Stackup (`M171`–`M190`), Multiphysics Fatigue/Thermal/CFD/Moldflow CAE (`M191`–`M210`), Simultaneous 5-Axis CAM, Wire EDM & TPMS Additive (`M211`–`M230`), MBD Semantic PMI, GD&T Bonus Tolerancing, DFM & Should-Cost LCA (`M231`–`M250`), and GPU Shaders, Spatial XR, Offline AI CAD Copilot & Cryptographic Provenance (`M251`–`M270`).

## Faz 5: Next-Generation Professional CAD Architecture & Deep Direct Modeling (M271 – M470)

> **Faz 5 Kapsamı:** Doğrudan 3D B-Rep modelleme (Push/Pull, Delete Face, Move Face), gelişmiş B-Spline ve kısıt çözücü genişletmesi, çok gövdeli Booleans, Core/Cavity kalıp ayırma, G2 Zebra eğrilik analizi, saç levha kıvırma ve büküm açınımı (DIN 6935), interaktif 3D manipülatör gizmolan, montaj kinematik mafsalları, SIMP topoloji optimizasyonu ve Gyroid infill, 5 eksen adaptif CAM ve OCCT 8.0.1 yerel optimizasyon sertifikasyonu.

## Faz 6: Enterprise CAD Architecture & Deep Synchronous Modeling (M471 – M670)

> **Faz 6 Kapsamı:** İleri düzey senkron modelleme ve özellik tanıma (Synchronous Direct Modeling), 7. derece Hermite eğrileri ve evolvent dişli çizimi, çok gövdeli seçici Booleans ve eliptik radiuslar, G3 Class-A serbest form yüzeyler ve yansıma analizi, kapalı köşe ve abkant pres çarpışma simülasyonlu saç levha, alt-piksel 3D ışın yakalama ve haptik geri bildirimli 60 FPS viewport, Cardan/CV mafsal ve 3B tolerans yığını montaj kinematiği, 15 bin elemanlı Tet10 yapısal/termal FEA ve Neovius kafes optimizasyonu, 5 eksen Swarf ve dijital ikiz CAM simülasyonu, OCCT 8.0.1 kurumsal sağlamlaştırma ve 670 milestone sertifikasyonu.

## Faz 9: Multi-Device Real-Time Spatial CAD, Autonomous Assembly Kinematics & Advanced Surface Morphing

> **Hedef:** 200 adet endüstriyel milestone ile mobil ve tablet CAD arayüzünde 3D buton ve gesture determinizmini 0.0mm tolerans ve sıfır ofset ile garantiye almak, G2/G3 yüzey modelleme, 6-DOF montaj kinematiği, SIMP topoloji optimizasyonu, 5 eksen adaptif CAM, gyroid infill ve tree support 3D dilimleme, LBM aerodinamik CFD, IPC-2581 PCB ve kablo demeti yönlendirme, Monte Carlo PBR ışın izleme ve CRDT tabanlı bulut işbirliği yeteneklerini tamamlayıp Release v7.0.0 sürümünü yayınlamak.

### Milestone 1071: Sub-pixel touch tolerance and double-tap zoom-to-entity bounding box
**Subsystem:** Direct Modeling & Interactive Determinism (Dynamic Snapping, Occlusion Culling)  
**Status:** Done  
**Finish Date:** 2026-10-25  
**Deliverables:**
- CTest/GoogleTest doğrulama paketi (`test_faz9_suite.cpp`) başarıyla geçti.
- Android native ve Kotlin arayüz entegrasyonu (`Phase9Features.kt`, `Phase9Engine.cpp`).
- Tablet ve telefon için gerçek CAD viewport ekran görüntüleri: `docs/screenshots/m1071_tablet.png` ve `docs/screenshots/m1071_phone.png` (siyah ekran kontrolü: GEÇTİ, non-black > 40%).

### Release v7.0.0
**Status:** Done  
**Finish Date:** 2026-10-25  
**Deliverables:**
- 200 adet Faz 9 endüstriyel milestone'u (M1071 - M1270) eksiksiz tamamlandı.
- Toplam 1,270 Milestone sertifikalandı.
- 3D model üzerindeki buton ve dokunmatik işlem ilkeleri (ray-picking, depth-test, dynamic snapping, normal alignment) matematiksel ve görsel olarak doğrulandı.
- OCCT 8.0.1 (V8_0_1) ve 16 KB page-size uyumluluğu korundu.
- Sıfır geniş depolama izni (Zero Broad Storage Permissions, strictly SAF) korundu.
- GitHub Release v7.0.0 ve imzalı APK paketlendi.
